([DigiStrique-Solutions/sentinel](https://github.com/DigiStrique-Solutions/sentinel/blob/HEAD/commands/loop.md) · ★1)
- **sentinel-onboard**: Guided team onboarding for new Sentinel users ([DigiStrique-Solutions/sentinel](https://github.com/DigiStrique-Solutions/sentinel/blob/HEAD/commands/onboard.md) · ★1)
- **sentinel-post**: Post-decision hygiene. Called 3-6 months after a recorded decision to review outcomes while correcting for the biases that contaminate retrospective judgment: Hindsight Bias (92), Choice-Supportive Bias (91), Outcome Bias (14), Self-Serving Bias (28), and Rosy Retrospection (94). The protocol forces evaluation of decision QUALITY separately from outcome QUALITY — these are not the same thing, and conflating them degrades future judgment. ([jamon8888/cc-suite](https://github.com/jamon8888/cc-suite/blob/HEAD/sentinel/commands/sentinel-post.md))
- **sentinel-promote**: Move a vault file from the repo vault to the global vault (for cross-repo knowledge). ([DigiStrique-Solutions/sentinel](https://github.com/DigiStrique-Solutions/sentinel/blob/HEAD/commands/promote.md) · ★1)
- **sentinel-prune**: Deep vault cleanup — find duplicates, validate cross-references, archive stale entries, and report vault health. Run monthly or before major feature pushes. ([DigiStrique-Solutions/sentinel](https://github.com/DigiStrique-Solutions/sentinel/blob/HEAD/commands/prune.md) · ★1)
